bash: {'command': 'cd /workdir/curator && cat > src/bespokelabs/curator/agent/processor.py << \'PYEOF\'\nimport datetime\nimport json\nimport os\nimport typing as t\n\nimport aiofiles\nimport aiohttp\nfrom datasets import Dataset\nfrom datasets.arrow_writer import ArrowWriter\n\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.agent.agent_response import AgentResponse\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.agent.turn_ledger import RESPONSES_FILENAME, TurnLedger, build_seed_record, load_ledger\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.log import logger\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.request_processor.online.base_online_request_processor import APIRequest\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.status_tracker.agent_status_tracker import AgentStatusTracker, AgentTurn\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.types.generic_response import GenericResponse\n\nif t.TYPE_CHECKING:\n    from bespokelabs.curator.agent.agent import Agent\n\n\nclass MultiTurnAgenticProcessor:\n    """A processor that manages multi-turn conversations between two agents.\n\n    This class handles the orchestration of conversations between a seeder agent\n    and a partner agent, managing the conversation history, caching, and dataset\n    creation. It supports both new conversations and resuming from cached states.\n\n    The conversation is a durable, resumable record: the seed is a real logged\n    turn and resume state is derived from the log via :class:`TurnLedger` rather\n    than from step parity, so a resumed run neither re-generates nor re-counts\n    what it already has.\n\n    Attributes:\n        seeder (Agent): The agent that initiates the conversation.\n        partner (Agent): The agent that responds to the seeder.\n        max_length (int): The budget of *generated responses* (not messages).\n        seed_message (str): The initial message to start the conversation.\n        conversation_history (list): List of message dictionaries containing role and content.\n        status_tracker (AgentStatusTracker): Tracks the status of the conversation.\n        ledger (Optional[TurnLedger]): The last ledger built for this run.\n    """\n\n    def __init__(self, seeder: "Agent", partner: "Agent", max_length: int, seed_message: str, now_fn: t.Callable[[], datetime.datetime] = datetime.datetime.now):\n        """Initialize a MultiTurnAgenticProcessor instance.\n\n        Args:\n            seeder (Agent): The agent that initiates the conversation.\n            partner (Agent): The agent that responds to the seeder.\n            max_length (int): The maximum number of turns in the conversation.\n            seed_message (str): The initial message to start the conversation.\n            now_fn: A callable returning the current ``datetime``, used to\n                timestamp the durable seed record. Defaults to\n                :func:`datetime.datetime.now`.\n        """\n        self.seeder = seeder\n        self.partner = partner\n        self.max_length = max_length\n        self.seed_message = seed_message\n        self.conversation_history: list[dict[str, str]] = []\n        self.now_fn = now_fn\n        self.ledger: t.Optional[TurnLedger] = None\n\n        # Create status tracker with model information\n        self.status_tracker = AgentStatusTracker(\n            seeder_name=self.seeder.name,\n            partner_name=self.partner.name,\n            max_turns=self.max_length,\n            seeder_model=self.seeder.model_name,  # Use seeder\'s model for cost tracking\n            partner_model=self.partner.model_name,  # Use partner\'s model for cost tracking\n        )\n\n    def _is_completed(self, author: str, content: t.Any) -> bool:\n        """Return whether ``author``\'s ``is_completed`` reports completion."""\n        agent = self._agent_for(author)\n        return bool(agent.is_completed(content))\n\n    def _agent_for(self, name: str) -> "Agent":\n        """Map an agent name to the seeder or partner agent.\n\n        Raises:\n            KeyError: If ``name`` is neither the seeder nor the partner.\n        """\n        if name == self.seeder.name:\n            return self.seeder\n        if name == self.partner.name:\n            return self.partner\n        raise KeyError(f"Unknown agent name: {name!r}")\n\n    def load_cache(self, working_dir: str) -> TurnLedger:\n        """Load the durable ledger for ``working_dir``.\n\n        Builds a :class:`TurnLedger` purely from the log on disk and never writes\n        to disk. The status tracker is intentionally *not* touched here; the\n        caller adopts the ledger separately. The processor keeps the last ledger\n        it built on :attr:`ledger`.\n\n        Args:\n            working_dir (str): Directory containing the cached conversation file.\n\n        Returns:\n            TurnLedger: The ledger describing the resumed conversation.\n        """\n        ledger = load_ledger(\n            working_dir,\n            seeder_name=self.seeder.name,\n            partner_name=self.partner.name,\n            max_responses=self.max_length,\n            is_completed=self._is_completed,\n        )\n        self.ledger = ledger\n        self.conversation_history = ledger.messages()\n        return ledger\n\n    async def run(self, working_dir: str) -> Dataset:\n        """Execute the multi-turn conversation between agents.\n\n        The seed is written as a real durable turn before the first request is\n        built. ``max_length`` is a budget of *generated responses*: the loop runs\n        ``while ledger.responses < self.max_length``, so an un-stopped run makes\n        exactly ``max_length`` calls to ``call_single_request`` and leaves\n        ``max_length + 1`` log lines whether it ran once or resumed five times.\n\n        Args:\n            working_dir (str): Directory where conversation results will be stored.\n\n        Returns:\n            Dataset: A HuggingFace Dataset containing the conversation history.\n        """\n        request_file = os.path.join(working_dir, RESPONSES_FILENAME)\n\n        # The seed is a durable turn. When the log is absent or empty we write\n        # exactly one line -- the seed record -- before the first request.\n        need_seed = not (os.path.exists(request_file) and os.path.getsize(request_file) > 0)\n        if need_seed:\n            seed_record = build_seed_record(\n                seeder_name=self.seeder.name,\n                seed_message=self.seed_message,\n                model_name=self.seeder.model_name,\n                now=self.now_fn(),\n            )\n            async with aiofiles.open(request_file, "a") as f:\n                await self.append_response(self.seeder.name, f, seed_record)\n\n        # Derive resume state from the log, never from step parity.\n        ledger = self.load_cache(working_dir)\n\n        # Adopt the ledger exactly once, before the completion short-circuit.\n        self.status_tracker.adopt_ledger(cached_responses=ledger.responses)\n\n        # Short-circuit on completion, never on a message count.\n        if ledger.completed:\n            self.status_tracker.stop_tracker()\n            return Dataset.from_file(self.create_dataset_file(working_dir))\n\n        async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:\n            async with aiofiles.open(request_file, "a") as f:\n                while ledger.responses < self.max_length:\n                    speaker = ledger.next_speaker\n                    assert speaker is not None  # noqa: S101 - invariant: not completed means there is a speaker\n                    agent = self._agent_for(speaker)\n                    agent_turn = AgentTurn.SEEDER if speaker == self.seeder.name else AgentTurn.PARTNER\n                    try:\n                        generic_request = self._transform_conversation_history(agent)\n                        api_request = APIRequest(\n                            task_id=ledger.responses,\n                            generic_request=generic_request,\n                            api_specific_request=agent._request_processor.create_api_specific_request_online(generic_request),\n                            attempts_left=1,\n                            prompt_formatter=agent.prompt_formatter,\n                        )\n                        response = await agent._request_processor.call_single_request(api_request, session, status_tracker=None)\n                        await self.append_response(agent.name, f, response)\n                        # Rebuild the ledger from the log so resume state stays derived.\n                        ledger = self.load_cache(working_dir)\n                        self.conversation_history = ledger.messages()\n                        self.status_tracker.update_turn(\n                            agent_turn,\n                            token_usage=response.token_usage,\n                            cost=response.response_cost,\n                        )\n                        if ledger.completed:\n                            break\n                    except Exception:\n                        # A failed turn appends nothing; record it against the\n                        # agent named by the ledger\'s next_speaker, not by parity.\n                        self.status_tracker.update_turn(agent_turn, response_success=False)\n                        raise\n\n        self.status_tracker.stop_tracker()\n        return Dataset.from_file(self.create_dataset_file(working_dir))\n\n    async def append_response(self, name: str, f, response: GenericResponse) -> None:\n        """Append a response to the conversation history file.\n\n        Args:\n            name (str): The name of the agent generating the response.\n            f: The file object to write to.\n            response (GenericResponse): The response to append.\n        """\n        response = response.model_dump()\n        response["name"] = name\n        await f.write(json.dumps(response, default=str) + "\\n")\n\n    def _transform_conversation_history(self, target_agent: "Agent"):\n        """Transform the conversation history into a request for ``target_agent``.\n\n        Every ledger message is mapped to ``{"role": "assistant"}`` when its\n        author equals ``target.name`` and ``{"role": "user"}`` otherwise --\n        uniformly, with no special case for a one-message history. The last mapped\n        message is replaced with the body produced by the target\'s prompt\n        formatter for the last message\'s content, and a single leading system\n        message (if any) is inserted at index 0. When the formatter produced more\n        than one system message, the first is used and the others stay in place.\n\n        Args:\n            target_agent (Agent): The agent for whom the conversation history is being transformed.\n\n        Returns:\n            GenericRequest: The transformed request.\n        """\n        target_name = target_agent.name\n        transformed_conversation_history = [\n            {"role": "assistant" if message["role"] == target_name else "user", "content": message["content"]}\n            for message in self.conversation_history\n        ]\n\n        request = target_agent.prompt_formatter.create_generic_request(\n            {"prompt": transformed_conversation_history[-1]["content"]}, 0\n        )\n        system_messages = [msg for msg in request.messages if msg["role"] == "system"]\n        body = [msg for msg in request.messages if msg["role"] != "system"]\n\n        # Replace the last mapped message with the formatter\'s body.\n        del transformed_conversation_history[-1]\n        transformed_conversation_history += body\n        request.messages = transformed_conversation_history\n\n        # Insert a single system message at index 0 only if there is one.\n        if system_messages:\n            request.messages.insert(0, system_messages[0])\n\n        return request\n\n    def create_dataset_file(self, working_dir: str) -> str:\n        """Create a dataset file from the conversation history.\n\n        One row is written per log line with exactly four fields:\n        ``{"role", "content", "turn", "source"}``. ``turn`` is the 0-based line\n        index and ``source`` is ``"seed"`` for index 0 and ``"response"``\n        otherwise -- decided by position, not by ``finish_reason``. Row order is\n        log order.\n\n        Args:\n            working_dir (str): Directory where the dataset file will be created.\n\n        Returns:\n            str: Path to the created dataset file.\n        """\n        response_file = os.path.join(working_dir, RESPONSES_FILENAME)\n        dataset_file = os.path.join(working_dir, "dataset.arrow")\n\n        with ArrowWriter(path=dataset_file) as writer:\n            with open(response_file, "r") as f_in:\n                for index, line in enumerate(f_in):\n                    response = AgentResponse.model_validate_json(line)\n                    row = response.model_dump()\n                    writer.write(\n                        {\n                            "role": row["name"],\n                            "content": str(row["response_message"]),\n                            "turn": index,\n                            "source": "seed" if index == 0 else "response",\n                        }\n                    )\n\n            # Finalize the writer\n            writer.finalize()\n\n        return dataset_file\nPYEOF\necho "written"'}

## [assistant]

The corrupt error now reports line_number 4 (physical line, 1-based, after skipping lines 2 and 3 which are blank/whitespace). The reason is the pydantic error string. 

Now there's a concern: the `is_completed` signature. The ticket says `build_ledger(..., is_completed: t.Callable[[str, t.Any], bool])`. My `_is_completed(author, content)` calls `agent.is_completed(content)`. The stock `Agent.is_completed` takes `(self, response)`. Good. But note the existing `is_completed` in tests/example takes `response: str`. My default uses `COMPLETION_SENTINEL in (response or "")`. 

Now there's a subtle issue: the `is_completed` is called on the last entry only, with the last entry's author. But what about the seed turn (turn 0)? The seed is authored by seeder. If the seed message contained the sentinel... but seed content is the seed_message; `is_completed` would be checked on the last entry. If only seed present, last entry is seed authored by seeder, content = seed_message. If seed_message contained sentinel, it would mark agent_signal. That's per spec ("checked on the last entry only"). Fine.

Now let me write a processor-level test that mocks `call_single_request` to verify the run loop, resume, and dataset file. Let me build a fake agent andrequest processor:

When the formatter produced more than one system message, the first is used and the others stay in place in the body."

Now `create_generic_request` uses `_put_system_prompt` which raises `ValueError` if a system message already exists in messages AND the formatter has a system_prompt. But for our transformed history, the prompt_func returns messages without system prompts (just `[{"role":"user","content":...}]`), and `_put_system_prompt` inserts the agent's system_prompt at front. So there will be exactly one system message normally. The multi-system case only happens if the prompt_func itself returns a system message AND the agent has a system_prompt — which `_put_system_prompt` would raise on. So in practice there's at most one system message. But my code handles the multi case correctly per spec. Good.

It supports both new conversations and resuming from cached states.\\n\\n    The conversation is a durable, resumable record: the seed is a real logged\\n    turn and resume state is derived from the log via :class:`TurnLedger` rather\\n    than from step parity, so a resumed run neither re-generates nor re-counts\\n    what it already has.\\n\\n    Attributes:\\n        seeder (Agent): The agent that initiates the conversation.\\n        partner (Agent): The agent that responds to the seeder.\\n        max_length (int): The budget of *generated responses* (not messages).\\n        seed_message (str): The initial message to start the conversation.\\n        conversation_history (list): List of message dictionaries containing role and content.\\n        status_tracker (AgentStatusTracker): Tracks the status of the conversation.\\n        ledger (Optional[TurnLedger]): The last ledger built for this run.\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, seeder: \\"Agent\\", partner: \\"Agent\\", max_length: int, seed_message: str, now_fn: t.Callable[[], datetime.datetime] = datetime.datetime.now):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Initialize a MultiTurnAgenticProcessor instance.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            seeder (Agent): The agent that initiates the conversation.\\n            partner (Agent): The agent that responds to the seeder.\\n            max_length (int): The maximum number of turns in the conversation.\\n            seed_message (str): The initial message to start the conversation.\\n            now_fn: A callable returning the current ``datetime``, used to\\n                timestamp the durable seed record. Defaults to\\n                :func:`datetime.datetime.now`.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        self.seeder = seeder\\n        self.partner = partner\\n        self.max_length = max_length\\n        self.seed_message = seed_message\\n        self.conversation_history: list[dict[str, str]] = []\\n        self.now_fn = now_fn\\n        self.ledger: t.Optional[TurnLedger] = None\\n\\n        # Create status tracker with model information\\n        self.status_tracker = AgentStatusTracker(\\n            seeder_name=self.seeder.name,\\n            partner_name=self.partner.name,\\n            max_turns=self.max_length,\\n            seeder_model=self.seeder.model_name,  # Use seeder\'s model for cost tracking\\n            partner_model=self.partner.model_name,  # Use partner\'s model for cost tracking\\n        )\\n\\n    def _is_completed(self, author: str, content: t.Any) -> bool:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Return whether ``author``\'s ``is_completed`` reports completion.\\"\\"\\"\\n        agent = self._agent_for(author)\\n        return bool(agent.is_completed(content))\\n\\n    def _agent_for(self, name: str) -> \\"Agent\\":\\n        \\"\\"\\"Map an agent name to the seeder or partner agent.\\n\\n        Raises:\\n            KeyError: If ``name`` is neither the seeder nor the partner.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if name == self.seeder.name:\\n            return self.seeder\\n        if name == self.partner.name:\\n            return self.partner\\n        raise KeyError(f\\"Unknown agent name: {name!r}\\")\\n\\n    def load_cache(self, working_dir: str) -> TurnLedger:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Load the durable ledger for ``working_dir``.\\n\\n        Builds a :class:`TurnLedger` purely from the log on disk and never writes\\n        to disk. The status tracker is intentionally *not* touched here; the\\n        caller adopts the ledger separately. The processor keeps the last ledger\\n        it built on :attr:`ledger`.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            working_dir (str): Directory containing the cached conversation file.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            TurnLedger: The ledger describing the resumed conversation.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        ledger = load_ledger(\\n            working_dir,\\n            seeder_name=self.seeder.name,\\n            partner_name=self.partner.name,\\n            max_responses=self.max_length,\\n            is_completed=self._is_completed,\\n        )\\n        self.ledger = ledger\\n        self.conversation_history = ledger.messages()\\n        return ledger\\n\\n    async def run(self, working_dir: str) -> Dataset:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Execute the multi-turn conversation between agents.\\n\\n        The seed is written as a real durable turn before the first request is\\n        built. ``max_length`` is a budget of *generated responses*: the loop runs\\n        ``while ledger.responses < self.max_length``, so an un-stopped run makes\\n        exactly ``max_length`` calls to ``call_single_request`` and leaves\\n        ``max_length + 1`` log lines whether it ran once or resumed five times.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            working_dir (str): Directory where conversation results will be stored.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Dataset: A HuggingFace Dataset containing the conversation history.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        request_file = os.path.join(working_dir, RESPONSES_FILENAME)\\n\\n        # The seed is a durable turn. When the log is absent or empty we write\\n        # exactly one line -- the seed record -- before the first request.\\n        need_seed = not (os.path.exists(request_file) and os.path.getsize(request_file) > 0)\\n        if need_seed:\\n            seed_record = build_seed_record(\\n                seeder_name=self.seeder.name,\\n                seed_message=self.seed_message,\\n                model_name=self.seeder.model_name,\\n                now=self.now_fn(),\\n            )\\n            async with aiofiles.open(request_file, \\"a\\") as f:\\n                await self.append_response(self.seeder.name, f, seed_record)\\n\\n        # Derive resume state from the log, never from step parity.\\n        ledger = self.load_cache(working_dir)\\n\\n        # Adopt the ledger exactly once, before the completion short-circuit.\\n        self.status_tracker.adopt_ledger(cached_responses=ledger.responses)\\n\\n        # Short-circuit on completion, never on a message count.\\n        if ledger.completed:\\n            self.status_tracker.stop_tracker()\\n            return Dataset.from_file(self.create_dataset_file(working_dir))\\n\\n        async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:\\n            async with aiofiles.open(request_file, \\"a\\") as f:\\n                while ledger.responses < self.max_length:\\n                    speaker = ledger.next_speaker\\n                    assert speaker is not None  # noqa: S101 - invariant: not completed means there is a speaker\\n                    agent = self._agent_for(speaker)\\n                    agent_turn = AgentTurn.SEEDER if speaker == self.seeder.name else AgentTurn.PARTNER\\n                    try:\\n                        generic_request = self._transform_conversation_history(agent)\\n                        api_request = APIRequest(\\n                            task_id=ledger.responses,\\n                            generic_request=generic_request,\\n                            api_specific_request=agent._request_processor.create_api_specific_request_online(generic_request),\\n                            attempts_left=1,\\n                            prompt_formatter=agent.prompt_formatter,\\n                        )\\n                        response = await agent._request_processor.call_single_request(api_request, session, status_tracker=None)\\n                        await self.append_response(agent.name, f, response)\\n                        # Make the durable write visible before re-deriving state from the log.\\n                        await f.flush()\\n                        # Rebuild the ledger from the log so resume state stays derived.\\n                        ledger = self.load_cache(working_dir)\\n                        self.conversation_history = ledger.messages()\\n                        self.status_tracker.update_turn(\\n                            agent_turn,\\n                            token_usage=response.token_usage,\\n                            cost=response.response_cost,\\n                        )\\n                        if ledger.completed:\\n                            break\\n                    except Exception:\\n                        # A failed turn appends nothing; record it against the\\n                        # agent named by the ledger\'s next_speaker, not by parity.\\n                        self.status_tracker.update_turn(agent_turn, response_success=False)\\n                        raise\\n\\n        self.status_tracker.stop_tracker()\\n        return Dataset.from_file(self.create_dataset_file(working_dir))\\n\\n    async def append_response(self, name: str, f, response: GenericResponse) -> None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Append a response to the conversation history file.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            name (str): The name of the agent generating the response.\\n            f: The file object to write to.\\n            response (GenericResponse): The response to append.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        response = response.model_dump()\\n        response[\\"name\\"] = name\\n        await f.write(json.dumps(response, default=str) + \\"\\\\n\\")\\n\\n    def _transform_conversation_history(self, target_agent: \\"Agent\\"):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Transform the conversation history into a request for ``target_agent``.\\n\\n        Every ledger message is mapped to ``{\\"role\\": \\"assistant\\"}`` when its\\n        author equals ``target.name`` and ``{\\"role\\": \\"user\\"}`` otherwise --\\n        uniformly, with no special case for a one-message history. The last mapped\\n        message is replaced with the body produced by the target\'s prompt\\n        formatter for the last message\'s content, and a single leading system\\n        message (if any) is inserted at index 0. When the formatter produced more\\n        than one system message, the first is used and the others stay in place.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            target_agent (Agent): The agent for whom the conversation history is being transformed.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            GenericRequest: The transformed request.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        target_name = target_agent.name\\n        transformed_conversation_history = [\\n            {\\"role\\": \\"assistant\\" if message[\\"role\\"] == target_name else \\"user\\", \\"content\\": message[\\"content\\"]}\\n            for message in self.conversation_history\\n        ]\\n\\n        request = target_agent.prompt_formatter.create_generic_request(\\n            {\\"prompt\\": transformed_conversation_history[-1][\\"content\\"]}, 0\\n        )\\n        # Split off the first system message; any further system messages stay in\\n        # the body (\\"the rest\\") in place.\\n        system_message: t.Optional[dict] = None\\n        rest: list[dict] = []\\n        for msg in request.messages:\\n            if system_message is None and msg[\\"role\\"] == \\"system\\":\\n                system_message = msg\\n            else:\\n                rest.append(msg)\\n\\n        # Replace the last mapped message with the rest.\\n        del transformed_conversation_history[-1]\\n        transformed_conversation_history += rest\\n        request.messages = transformed_conversation_history\\n\\n        # Insert the (single) system message at index 0 only if there is one.\\n        if system_message is not None:\\n            request.messages.insert(0, system_message)\\n\\n        return request\\n\\n    def create_dataset_file(self, working_dir: str) -> str:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Create a dataset file from the conversation history.\\n\\n        One row is written per log line with exactly four fields:\\n        ``{\\"role\\", \\"content\\", \\"turn\\", \\"source\\"}``. ``turn`` is the 0-based line\\n        index and ``source`` is ``\\"seed\\"`` for index 0 and ``\\"response\\"``\\n        otherwise -- decided by position, not by ``finish_reason``. Row order is\\n        log order.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            working_dir (str): Directory where the dataset file will be created.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            str: Path to the created dataset file.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        response_file = os.path.join(working_dir, RESPONSES_FILENAME)\\n        dataset_file = os.path.join(working_dir, \\"dataset.arrow\\")\\n\\n        with ArrowWriter(path=dataset_file) as writer:\\n            with open(response_file, \\"r\\") as f_in:\\n                for index, line in enumerate(f_in):\\n                    response = AgentResponse.model_validate_json(line)\\n                    row = response.model_dump()\\n                    writer.write(\\n                        {\\n                            \\"role\\": row[\\"name\\"],\\n                            \\"content\\": str(row[\\"response_message\\"]),\\n                            \\"turn\\": index,\\n                            \\"source\\": \\"seed\\" if index == 0 else \\"response\\",\\n                        }\\n                    )\\n\\n            # Finalize the writer\\n            writer.finalize()\\n\\n        return dataset_file\\n\\n\\n",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None isError=False

## Summary

I implemented the durable, resumable multi-turn agent conversation record. Here's what changed:

### New module: `src/bespokelabs/curator/agent/turn_ledger.py`
- Module docstring `"""The durable record of a multi-turn agent conversation."""`; imports only `datetime`, `os`, `typing`, `dataclasses` (no `time`/`random`/`uuid`/`os.urandom`).
- Constants: `RESPONSES_FILENAME`, `SEED_FINISH_REASON="seed"`, `COMPLETION_SENTINEL`, `COMPLETION_REASONS`, `LEDGER_STATUSES`.
- Exceptions: `TurnLedgerError(RuntimeError)` and `TurnLedgerCorruptError(TurnLedgerError)` with `__init__(self, path, line_number, reason)` storing `.path`/`.line_number` (1-based physical)/`.reason`.
- Two **frozen dataclasses** with exact field order: `TurnEntry(turn, author, content, source)` and `TurnLedger(entries, seeder_name, partner_name, max_responses, responses, turns, next_speaker, last_author, interleave_faults, completed, completion_reason, status)` (`entries` is a tuple). `TurnLedger.messages()` returns `{"role","content"}` dicts in log order.
- Functions: `build_seed_record` (returns an `AgentResponse` via `model_validate`), `read_log` (skips blank lines, wraps parse errors as `TurnLedgerCorruptError`), `build_ledger` (derives all state from entries â `responses==turns-1`, `next_speaker` is the complement of the last author never parity, `interleave_faults` counts repeated authors, `completion_reason` = agent_signal > budget > open, empty log is always `"open"`), and `load_ledger` (read + build, never writes).

### `MultiTurnAgenticProcessor` (`agent/processor.py`)
- `__init__` keeps `self.now_fn` and `self.ledger=None`; status tracker unchanged.
- `load_cache` returns a `TurnLedger` (built from the log via `load_ledger`), never writes, never touches the status tracker; stores the ledger on `self.ledger` and syncs `conversation_history`.
- `run()` writes the seed as a real `AgentResponse` line via `append_response()` before the first request when the log is absent/empty; loads the ledger; calls `adopt_ledger(cached_responses=ledger.responses)` **once** before the `ledger.completed` short-circuit; loops `while ledger.responses < self.max_length`, asking `ledger.next_speaker` (via `_agent_for`, raising `KeyError` for unknown names), with `APIRequest(task_id=ledger.responses)`; after each turn flushes, rebuilds the ledger from the log, syncs `conversation_history=ledger.messages()`, and breaks on `ledger.completed`. Failed turns append nothing, are recorded against `next_speaker`, and re-raise.
- `_transform_conversation_history` maps every message uniformly (`assistant` if author==target else `user`, no one-message special case), replaces the last mapped message with the formatter body, inserts the first system message at index 0 only if present (extra system messages stay in the body).
- `create_dataset_file` writes 4 fields per log line (`role`, `content`, `turn`, `source` by position).

### `AgentStatusTracker` (`status_tracker/agent_status_tracker.py`)
- New fields `num_cached=0` and `time_fn=field(default=time.time, repr=False, compare=False)`; `start_time`/`last_update_time` derived from `time_fn` in `__post_init__` (and all `time.time()` calls now use `self.time_fn()`).
- `update_turn` increments only `num_errors` (leaves `current_turn`/`num_responses` alone), still sets `current_agent` and accumulates tokens/cost.
- New `adopt_ledger(*, cached_responses)` sets `current_turn=cached_responses`, `num_cached=cached_responses`, resets `num_responses`/`num_errors`/`total_cost`/token counters; doesn't touch `max_turns` or call `update_display`.
- `stop_tracker()` pops `"time_fn"` from telemetry metadata alongside `"pbar"`.

### `Agent` / `MultiTurnAgents` (`agent/agent.py`)
- `Agent.is_completed` now has a real default: `COMPLETION_SENTINEL in (response or "")`.
- `MultiTurnAgents.__init__` accepts and forwards `now_fn` (keyword-only, default `datetime.now`).

### `MultiTurnResponse` (`agent/agent_response.py`)
- `update_tracker_stats` reports `RequestStats(total=tracker.max_turns, succeeded=tracker.num_responses, failed=tracker.num_errors, in_progress=0, cached=tracker.num_cached)`.

### Integration test (`tests/integrations/test_agent.py`)
- Updated to the new contract: `max_length + 1` rows (5), `role/content/turn/source` columns, seed row first, and recomputed content hash (`aa73426f...`) since the seed is now a durable logged turn. VCR matching is URI+method only, and the call order (partner, seeder, ...) is unchanged, so the cassette still replays.

No changes to `llm/`, `request_processor/`, `client.py`, `db.py`, the viewer, `Agent._hash_fingerprint`, or the `xxh64(seed_message)` run identity. No new dependencies.
